    13	namespace {
    14	
    15	// A field contains one halo plane on each side of the z slab.  The owned
    16	// planes are [1, localNz], which makes the stencil inner loop contiguous.
    17	inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
    18	                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    19	    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
    20	}
    21	
    22	struct Slab {
    23	    size_t firstGlobalZ;
    24	    size_t localNz;
    25	    size_t planeSize;
    26	    int previous;
    27	    int next;
    28	};
    29	
    30	// Start the nearest-neighbor exchange needed by the z part of the stencil.
    31	// The two physical boundaries use the same clamped values as the serial code.
    32	void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
    33	                       MPI_Comm comm, MPI_Request requests[4], int& requestCount) {
    34	    const size_t plane = slab.planeSize;
    35	    double* const firstOwned = field.data() + plane;
    36	    double* const lastOwned = field.data() + slab.localNz * plane;
    37	
    38	    if (slab.previous == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
    39	        std::copy_n(firstOwned, plane, field.data());
    40	    }
    41	    if (slab.next == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
    42	        std::copy_n(lastOwned, plane, field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane);
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    requestCount = 0;
    46	    if (slab.previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
    47	        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
    48	                  1, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
    49	        MPI_Isend(firstOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
    50	                  0, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
    51	    }
    52	    if (slab.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
    53	        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane,
    54	                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 0, comm,
    55	                  &requests[requestCount++]);
    56	        MPI_Isend(lastOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 1,
    57	                  comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
    58	    }
    59	}
    60	
    61	void finishHaloExchange(MPI_Request requests[4], const int requestCount) {
    62	    if (requestCount != 0) {
    63	        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
    64	    }
    65	}
    66	
    67	inline void computeChemicalPotentialPlane(const std::vector<double>& c,
    68	                                          std::vector<double>& mu,
    69	                                          const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
    70	                                          const size_t z, const double dx,
    71	                                          const double dy, const double dz,
    72	                                          const double gamma, const double e_AA,
    73	                                          const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    74	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    75	    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
    76	    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
    77	    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
    78	    const double* const previous = c.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
    79	    const double* const current = c.data() + z * plane;
    80	    const double* const next = c.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
    81	    double* const output = mu.data() + z * plane;
    82	
    83	    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
    84	        const size_t row = y * nx;
    85	        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
    86	        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
    87	        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
    88	            const size_t point = row + x;
    89	            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
    90	            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
    91	            const double cv = current[point];
    92	
    93	            const double cxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
    94	                                2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
    95	            const double cyy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
    96	                                2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
    97	            const double czz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
    98	            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
    99	
   100	            output[point] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB -
   101	                                   2.0 * cv * e_AB) +
   102	                            3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
   103	        }
   104	    }
   105	}
   106	
   107	inline void updateConcentrationPlane(std::vector<double>& cnew,
   108	                                     const std::vector<double>& cold,
   109	                                     const std::vector<double>& mu,
   110	                                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
   111	                                     const size_t z, const double D,
   112	                                     const double dt, const double dx,
   113	                                     const double dy, const double dz) {
   114	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
   115	    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
   116	    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
   117	    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
   118	    const double* const previous = mu.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
   119	    const double* const current = mu.data() + z * plane;
   120	    const double* const next = mu.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
   121	    const double* const oldValues = cold.data() + z * plane;
   122	    double* const output = cnew.data() + z * plane;
   123	
   124	    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
   125	        const size_t row = y * nx;
   126	        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
   127	        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
   128	        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
   129	            const size_t point = row + x;
   130	            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
   131	            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
   132	            const double value = current[point];
   133	
   134	            const double mxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
   135	                                2.0 * value) * invDx2;
   136	            const double myy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
   137	                                2.0 * value) * invDy2;
   138	            const double mzz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
   139	            output[point] = oldValues[point] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
   140	        }
   141	    }
   142	}
   143	
   144	void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx,
   145	                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
   146	                             const Slab& slab) {
   147	    const size_t volume = nx * ny * globalNz;
   148	    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= slab.localNz; ++localZ) {
   149	        const size_t globalZ = slab.firstGlobalZ + localZ - 1;
   150	        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
   151	            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
   152	                const size_t localIndex = idx3(x, y, localZ, nx, ny);
   153	                const size_t linearId = globalZ * slab.planeSize + y * nx + x;
   154	                const double pseudo = (((linearId + 1) * 1299709) % volume) /
   155	                                      static_cast<double>(volume);
   156	                c[localIndex] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   157	            }
   158	        }
   159	    }
   160	}
   161	
   162	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, const Slab& slab,
   163	                    MPI_Comm comm, const int rank) {
   164	    const double* const values = c.data() + slab.planeSize;
   165	    const size_t count = slab.localNz * slab.planeSize;
   166	    int localInvalid = 0;
   167	    double localMin = values[0];
   168	    double localMax = values[0];
   169	    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   170	        if (!std::isfinite(values[i])) {
   171	            localInvalid = 1;
   172	            break;
   173	        }
   174	        localMin = std::min(localMin, values[i]);
   175	        localMax = std::max(localMax, values[i]);
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    int globalInvalid = 0;
   179	    MPI_Allreduce(&localInvalid, &globalInvalid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
   180	    if (globalInvalid != 0) {
   181	        if (rank == 0) {
   182	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   183	        }
   184	        return false;
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    double globalMin = 0.0;
   188	    double globalMax = 0.0;
   189	    MPI_Reduce(&localMin, &globalMin, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, comm);
   190	    MPI_Reduce(&localMax, &globalMax, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
   191	    int rangeValid = 1;
   192	    if (rank == 0) {
   193	        printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", globalMin, globalMax);
   194	        if (globalMax > 10.0 || globalMin < -10.0) {
   195	            printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   196	            rangeValid = 0;
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	    MPI_Bcast(&rangeValid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
   200	    return rangeValid != 0;
   201	}
   202	
   203	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   204	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   205	    printf("Options:\n");
   206	    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   207	    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   208	    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   209	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   210	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   211	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   212	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   213	}
   214	
   215	} // namespace
   216	
   217	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   218	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   219	
   220	    int worldRank = 0;
   221	    int worldSize = 0;
   222	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   223	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   224	
   225	    size_t nx = 64;
   226	    size_t ny = 0;
   227	    size_t nz = 0;
   228	    int iterations = 20;
   229	    bool validate = false;
   230	    bool printResults = false;
   231	
   232	    int parseStatus = 0;
   233	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   234	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   235	            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   236	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   237	            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
   238	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   239	            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
   240	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   241	            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
   242	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   243	            validate = true;
   244	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   245	            printResults = true;
   246	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   247	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   248	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   249	            }
   250	            MPI_Finalize();
   251	            return 0;
   252	        } else {
   253	            parseStatus = 1;
   254	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   255	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   256	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   257	            }
   258	            break;
   259	        }
   260	    }
   261	    if (parseStatus != 0) {
   262	        MPI_Finalize();
   263	        return 1;
   264	    }
   265	
   266	    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
   267	    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
   268	
   269	    const bool dimensionsInvalid =
   270	        nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 ||
   271	        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
   272	        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
   273	        nx * ny > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   274	    if (dimensionsInvalid) {
   275	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   276	            printf("Invalid grid dimensions for MPI decomposition\n");
   277	        }
   278	        MPI_Finalize();
   279	        return 1;
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;
   283	    const size_t gridSize = planeSize * nz;
   284	
   285	    // With more processes than z planes, ranks without cells leave the active
   286	    // communicator.  This preserves a real distributed decomposition for all
   287	    // useful ranks without ever allocating or exchanging empty slabs.
   288	    const int activeSize = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize) < nz
   289	                               ? worldSize
   290	                               : static_cast<int>(nz);
   291	    MPI_Comm activeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   292	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, worldRank < activeSize ? 1 : MPI_UNDEFINED,
   293	                   worldRank, &activeComm);
   294	    if (worldRank >= activeSize) {
   295	        MPI_Finalize();
   296	        return 0;
   297	    }
   298	
   299	    int rank = 0;
   300	    int processCount = 0;
   301	    MPI_Comm_rank(activeComm, &rank);
   302	    MPI_Comm_size(activeComm, &processCount);
   303	
   304	    const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(processCount);
   305	    const size_t extraPlanes = nz % static_cast<size_t>(processCount);
   306	    const size_t localNz = basePlanes + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < extraPlanes ? 1 : 0);
   307	    const size_t firstGlobalZ = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * basePlanes +
   308	                                std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extraPlanes);
   309	    const Slab slab{firstGlobalZ, localNz, planeSize,
   310	                    rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1,
   311	                    rank + 1 == processCount ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1};
   312	
   313	    if (rank == 0) {
   314	        printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
   315	        printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   316	        printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   317	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   318	    }
   319	
   320	    // Physical parameters
   321	    const double dx = 1.0;
   322	    const double dy = 1.0;
   323	    const double dz = 1.0;
   324	    const double dt = 0.01;
   325	    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   326	    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   327	    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
   328	    const double gamma = 0.5;
   329	    const double D = 1.0;
   330	
   331	    const size_t localFieldSize = (localNz + 2) * planeSize;
   332	    std::vector<double> cold(localFieldSize);
   333	    std::vector<double> cnew(localFieldSize);
   334	    std::vector<double> mu(localFieldSize);
   335	
   336	    if (rank == 0) {
   337	        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   338	    }
   339	    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz, slab);
   340	
   341	    if (rank == 0) {
   342	        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   343	    }
   344	    MPI_Barrier(activeComm);
   345	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   346	
   347	    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   348	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   349	        int requestCount = 0;
   350	        beginHaloExchange(cold, slab, activeComm, requests, requestCount);
   351	
   352	        // Interior planes do not depend on halo data and overlap communication.
   353	        for (size_t z = 2; z < localNz; ++z) {
   354	            computeChemicalPotentialPlane(cold, mu, nx, ny, z, dx, dy, dz,
   355	                                          gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
   356	        }
   357	        finishHaloExchange(requests, requestCount);
   358	        computeChemicalPotentialPlane(cold, mu, nx, ny, 1, dx, dy, dz,
   359	                                      gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
   360	        if (localNz > 1) {
   361	            computeChemicalPotentialPlane(cold, mu, nx, ny, localNz, dx, dy, dz,
   362	                                          gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
   363	        }
   364	
   365	        beginHaloExchange(mu, slab, activeComm, requests, requestCount);
   366	        for (size_t z = 2; z < localNz; ++z) {
   367	            updateConcentrationPlane(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, z, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   368	        }
   369	        finishHaloExchange(requests, requestCount);
   370	        updateConcentrationPlane(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, 1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   371	        if (localNz > 1) {
   372	            updateConcentrationPlane(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, localNz,
   373	                                     D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   374	        }
   375	        std::swap(cold, cnew);
   376	    }
   377	
   378	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   379	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   380	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, activeComm);

-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
+namespace {
+
+// A field contains one halo plane on each side of the z slab.  The owned
+// planes are [1, localNz], which makes the stencil inner loop contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
-    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
-    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
-    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
-    
-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
-    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] -

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstGlobalZ;
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t planeSize;
+    int previous;
+    int next;
+};
+
+// Start the nearest-neighbor exchange needed by the z part of the stencil.
+// The two physical boundaries use the same clamped values as the serial code.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       MPI_Comm comm, MPI_Request requests[4], int& requestCount) {
+    const size_t plane = slab.planeSize;
+    double* const firstOwned = field.data() + plane;
+    double* const lastOwned = field.data() + slab.localNz * plane;
+
+    if (slab.previous == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(firstOwned, plane, field.data());
+    }
+    if (slab.next == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(lastOwned, plane, field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane);
+    }
+
+    requestCount = 0;
+    if (slab.previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  1, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  0, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (slab.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 0, comm,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 1,
+                  comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+}
+
+void finishHaloExchange(MPI_Request requests[4], const int requestCount) {
+    if (requestCount != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
 }

-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv

+inline void computeChemicalPotentialPlane(const std::vector<double>& c,
+                                          std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                          const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                          const size_t z, const double dx,
+                                          const double dy, const double dz,
+                                          const double gamma, const double e_AA,
+                                          const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = c.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = c.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = c.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    double* const output = mu.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double cv = current[point];
+
+            const double cxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+            const double cyy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+            const double czz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
+
+            output[point] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB -
+                                   2.0 * cv * e_AB) +
+                            3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
         }
     }
 }

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

+inline void updateConcentrationPlane(std::vector<double>& cnew,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& cold,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                     const size_t z, const double D,
+                                     const double dt, const double dx,
+                                     const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = mu.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = mu.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = mu.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    const double* const oldValues = cold.data() + z * plane;
+    double* const output = cnew.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double value = current[point];
+
+            const double mxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+            const double myy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+            const double mzz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+            output[point] = oldValues[point] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
         }
     }
 }

+namespace {
+
+// A field contains one halo plane on each side of the z slab.  The owned
+// planes are [1, localNz], which makes the stencil inner loop contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] -

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstGlobalZ;
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t planeSize;
+    int previous;
+    int next;
+};
+
+// Start the nearest-neighbor exchange needed by the z part of the stencil.
+// The two physical boundaries use the same clamped values as the serial code.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       MPI_Comm comm, MPI_Request requests[4], int& requestCount) {
+    const size_t plane = slab.planeSize;
+    double* const firstOwned = field.data() + plane;
+    double* const lastOwned = field.data() + slab.localNz * plane;
+
+    if (slab.previous == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(firstOwned, plane, field.data());
+    }
+    if (slab.next == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(lastOwned, plane, field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane);
+    }
+
+    requestCount = 0;
+    if (slab.previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  1, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  0, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (slab.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 0, comm,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 1,
+                  comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+}
+
+void finishHaloExchange(MPI_Request requests[4], const int requestCount) {
+    if (requestCount != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
 }

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {

+inline void computeChemicalPotentialPlane(const std::vector<double>& c,
+                                          std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                          const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                          const size_t z, const double dx,
+                                          const double dy, const double dz,
+                                          const double gamma, const double e_AA,
+                                          const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = c.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = c.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = c.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    double* const output = mu.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double cv = current[point];
+
+            const double cxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+            const double cyy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+            const double czz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
+
+            output[point] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB -
+                                   2.0 * cv * e_AB) +
+                            3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);

+inline void updateConcentrationPlane(std::vector<double>& cnew,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& cold,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                     const size_t z, const double D,
+                                     const double dt, const double dx,
+                                     const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = mu.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = mu.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = mu.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    const double* const oldValues = cold.data() + z * plane;
+    double* const output = cnew.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double value = current[point];
+
+            const double mxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+            const double myy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+            const double mzz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+            output[point] = oldValues[point] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
         }
     }

+
+// A field contains one halo plane on each side of the z slab.  The owned
+// planes are [1, localNz], which makes the stencil inner loop contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;

-    
-    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
-    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstGlobalZ;
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t planeSize;
+    int previous;
+    int next;
+};
+
+// Start the nearest-neighbor exchange needed by the z part of the stencil.
+// The two physical boundaries use the same clamped values as the serial code.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       MPI_Comm comm, MPI_Request requests[4], int& requestCount) {
+    const size_t plane = slab.planeSize;
+    double* const firstOwned = field.data() + plane;
+    double* const lastOwned = field.data() + slab.localNz * plane;
+
+    if (slab.previous == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(firstOwned, plane, field.data());
+    }
+    if (slab.next == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(lastOwned, plane, field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane);
+    }
+
+    requestCount = 0;
+    if (slab.previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  1, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  0, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (slab.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 0, comm,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 1,
+                  comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+}
+
+void finishHaloExchange(MPI_Request requests[4], const int requestCount) {
+    if (requestCount != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
 }

+inline void computeChemicalPotentialPlane(const std::vector<double>& c,
+                                          std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                          const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                          const size_t z, const double dx,
+                                          const double dy, const double dz,
+                                          const double gamma, const double e_AA,
+                                          const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = c.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = c.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = c.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    double* const output = mu.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double cv = current[point];
+
+            const double cxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+            const double cyy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+            const double czz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
+
+            output[point] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB -
+                                   2.0 * cv * e_AB) +
+                            3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);

+inline void updateConcentrationPlane(std::vector<double>& cnew,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& cold,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                     const size_t z, const double D,
+                                     const double dt, const double dx,
+                                     const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = mu.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = mu.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = mu.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    const double* const oldValues = cold.data() + z * plane;
+    double* const output = cnew.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double value = current[point];
+
+            const double mxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+            const double myy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+            const double mzz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+            output[point] = oldValues[point] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
         }
     }
 }

+
+// A field contains one halo plane on each side of the z slab.  The owned
+// planes are [1, localNz], which makes the stencil inner loop contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
     return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstGlobalZ;
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t planeSize;
+    int previous;
+    int next;
+};
+
+// Start the nearest-neighbor exchange needed by the z part of the stencil.
+// The two physical boundaries use the same clamped values as the serial code.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       MPI_Comm comm, MPI_Request requests[4], int& requestCount) {
+    const size_t plane = slab.planeSize;
+    double* const firstOwned = field.data() + plane;
+    double* const lastOwned = field.data() + slab.localNz * plane;
+
+    if (slab.previous == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(firstOwned, plane, field.data());
+    }
+    if (slab.next == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(lastOwned, plane, field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane);
+    }
+
+    requestCount = 0;
+    if (slab.previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  1, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.previous,
+                  0, comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (slab.next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 0, comm,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwned, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, slab.next, 1,
+                  comm, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+}
+
+void finishHaloExchange(MPI_Request requests[4], const int requestCount) {
+    if (requestCount != 0) {
+        MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+    }
 }

+inline void computeChemicalPotentialPlane(const std::vector<double>& c,
+                                          std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                          const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                          const size_t z, const double dx,
+                                          const double dy, const double dz,
+                                          const double gamma, const double e_AA,
+                                          const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = c.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = c.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = c.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    double* const output = mu.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double cv = current[point];
+
+            const double cxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDx2;
+            const double cyy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * cv) * invDy2;
+            const double czz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * cv) * invDz2;
+            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
+
+            output[point] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB -
+                                   2.0 * cv * e_AB) +
+                            3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
         }
     }
 }

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

+inline void updateConcentrationPlane(std::vector<double>& cnew,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& cold,
+                                     const std::vector<double>& mu,
+                                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                                     const size_t z, const double D,
+                                     const double dt, const double dx,
+                                     const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
+    const double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
+    const double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
+    const double* const previous = mu.data() + (z - 1) * plane;
+    const double* const current = mu.data() + z * plane;
+    const double* const next = mu.data() + (z + 1) * plane;
+    const double* const oldValues = cold.data() + z * plane;
+    double* const output = cnew.data() + z * plane;
+
+    for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = y * nx;
+        const size_t previousRow = (y == 0 ? y : y - 1) * nx;
+        const size_t nextRow = (y + 1 < ny ? y + 1 : y) * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t point = row + x;
+            const size_t previousX = x == 0 ? x : x - 1;
+            const size_t nextX = x + 1 < nx ? x + 1 : x;
+            const double value = current[point];
+
+            const double mxx = (current[row + nextX] + current[row + previousX] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDx2;
+            const double myy = (current[nextRow + x] + current[previousRow + x] -
+                                2.0 * value) * invDy2;
+            const double mzz = (next[point] + previous[point] - 2.0 * value) * invDz2;
+            output[point] = oldValues[point] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);
         }
     }
 }
